- [ ] `lib/edit/r_info.txt:17449` [record] id=774 Dreadlord | G@17450=G:r; I@17451=120:30d100:20:150:10; W@17452=62:2:0:20000; E@17453=0:0:0:0:0:0; O@17454=10:10:60:10; B@17455=HIT:EXP_40:6d6; B@17456=HIT:EXP_40:6d6; B@17457=HIT:LOSE_STR:4d6; B@17458=HIT:LOSE_STR:4d6; F@17459=CAN_FLY; F@17460=COLD_BLOOD; F@17461=DROP_2D2; F@17462=DROP_3D2; F@17463=DROP_4D2; F@17464=EVIL; F@17465=FORCE_MAXHP; F@17466=FORCE_SLEEP; F@17467=IM_COLD; F@17468=IM_POIS; F@17469=INVISIBLE; F@17470=NO_CONF; F@17471=NO_CUT; F@17472=NO_SLEEP; F@17473=ONLY_ITEM; F@17474=PASS_WALL; F@17475=RES_TELE; F@17476=TAKE_ITEM; F@17477=UNDEAD; S@17478=1_IN_3; S@17479=BA_NETH; S@17480=BLIND; S@17481=BRAIN_SMASH; S@17482=CONF; S@17483=DARKNESS; S@17484=DRAIN_MANA; S@17485=HOLD; S@17486=SCARE; S@17487=S_UNDEAD; S@17488=TELE_TO; S@17489=TPORT; D@17490=It is a massive form of animated death, its colour deeper than black.  It ; D@17491=drinks in light, and space around it is twisted and torn by the weight of ; D@17492=its evil.  It is unlife and it knows nothing but the stealing of souls and ; D@17493=the stench of death.  Flee its hunger!

Do not think that since he loves the novels of ; D@19710=Tolkien he is to be ignored! He wishes to translate your soul into ; D@19711=assembler, the one hitch being that he must kill you first. If you ; D@19712=encounter him, pray your deity holds you in good stead. 
- [ ] `lib/edit/r_info.txt:19714` [record] id=862 Morgoth, Lord of Darkness | G@19715=P:D; I@19716=140:200d150:100:150:0; W@19717=100:1:200000:60000; E@19718=1:1:1:2:1:1; O@19719=0:100:0:0; B@19720=HIT:SHATTER:24d10; B@19721=HIT:SHATTER:24d10; B@19722=HIT:LOSE_ALL:10d12; B@19723=TOUCH:UN_POWER; F@19724=AURA_COLD; F@19725=CAN_SPEAK; F@19726=DROP_1D2; F@19727=DROP_2D2; F@19728=DROP_3D2; F@19729=DROP_4D2; F@19730=DROP_CHOSEN; F@19731=DROP_GOOD; F@19732=DROP_GREAT; F@19733=EVIL; F@19734=FORCE_DEPTH; F@19735=FORCE_MAXHP; F@19736=FORCE_SLEEP; F@19737=HAS_LITE; F@19738=IM_ACID; F@19739=IM_COLD; F@19740=IM_ELEC; F@19741=IM_FIRE; F@19742=IM_POIS; F@19743=KILL_WALL; F@19744=MALE; F@19745=MOVE_BODY; F@19746=NO_CONF; F@19747=NO_FEAR; F@19748=NO_SLEEP; F@19749=NO_STUN; F@19750=ONLY_ITEM; F@19751=POWERFUL; F@19752=REGENERATE; F@19753=RES_NETH; F@19754=RES_TELE; F@19755=SMART; F@19756=UNIQUE; S@19757=1_IN_3; S@19758=BA_CHAO; S@19759=BA_DARK; S@19760=BA_MANA; S@19761=BA_NETH; S@19762=BO_MANA; S@19763=BRAIN_SMASH; S@19764=BR_DISI; S@19765=BR_NETH; S@19766=HAND_DOOM; S@19767=ROCKET; S@19768=S_HI_DEMON; S@19769=S_HI_DRAGON; S@19770=S_HI_UNDEAD; S@19771=S_MONSTERS; S@19772=S_UNIQUE; S@19773=S_WRAITH; D@19774=He was the most powerful of the Valar, the equal of Manwe. ; D@19775=He is the Master of the Pits of Angband. His figure is like a black ; D@19776=mountain crowned with Lightning. He rages with everlasting anger, his ; D@19777=body scarred by Fingolfin's eight mighty wounds. He can never rest from ; D@19778=his pain, but seeks forever to dominate all that is light and good in the ; D@19779=world. He is the origin of man's fear of darkness and created many foul ; D@19780=creatures with his evil powers. Orcs, Dragons, and Trolls are his most ; D@19781=foul corruptions, causing much pain and suffering in the world to please ; D@19782=him. His disgusting visage, twisted with evil, is crowned with iron, the ; D@19783=two remaining Silmarils forever burning him. Grond, the mighty Hammer of ; D@19784=the Underworld, cries defiance as he strides towards you to crush you to a ; D@19785=pulp!

- [ ] `lib/edit/r_info.txt:24117` [record] id=1032 Tik'srvzllat | G@24118=G:v; I@24119=142:180d100:200:170:0; W@24120=127:2:1000:350000; E@24121=1:1:1:2:1:1; O@24122=50:0:50:0; B@24123=GAZE:UN_BONUS:10d10; B@24124=GAZE:TIME:10d10; B@24125=GAZE:INSANITY:10d10; B@24126=GAZE:INSANITY:10d5; F@24127=AURA_COLD; F@24128=COLD_BLOOD; F@24129=DROP_3D2; F@24130=DROP_4D2; F@24131=DROP_GOOD; F@24132=DROP_GREAT; F@24133=EVIL; F@24134=FORCE_MAXHP; F@24135=IM_ACID; F@24136=IM_COLD; F@24137=IM_ELEC; F@24138=IM_FIRE; F@24139=IM_POIS; F@24140=INVISIBLE; F@24141=NO_CUT; F@24142=ONLY_ITEM; F@24143=PASS_WALL; F@24144=POWERFUL; F@24145=RES_NETH; F@24146=SPECIAL_GENE; F@24147=UNDEAD; F@24148=UNIQUE; F@24149=WEIRD_MIND; S@24150=1_IN_2; S@24151=BA_NETH; S@24152=BR_DISE; S@24153=S_DEMON; S@24154=S_DRAGON; S@24155=S_HI_DEMON; S@24156=S_HI_DRAGON; S@24157=S_HI_UNDEAD; S@24158=S_KIN; S@24159=S_UNDEAD; S@24160=S_UNIQUE; S@24161=S_WRAITH; D@24162=A disembodied and barely sentient mind, Tik'srvzllat floated ; D@24163=through the void for eons before being awakened by sorcery, pulled ; D@24164=into the nether realm, and shaped into the being you see before you.  ; D@24165=A flickering purple outline of a sphere, with eerie yellow-purple ; D@24166=mist circling rapidly around it, Tik'srvzllat threatens your sanity ; D@24167=with its appearance alone. 
- [ ] `lib/edit/r_info.txt:24169` [record] id=1033 The Glass Golem | G@24170=g:W; I@24171=130:100d15:200:170:0; W@24172=52:4:0:2000; E@24173=0:0:0:0:0:0; O@24174=0:0:0:0; B@24175=HIT:INSANITY:5d6; B@24176=HIT:INSANITY:6d6; B@24177=HIT:INSANITY:7d6; B@24178=HIT:INSANITY:6d6; F@24179=BASH_DOOR; F@24180=CAN_FLY; F@24181=CHAR_MULTI; F@24182=COLD_BLOOD; F@24183=EMPTY_MIND; F@24184=ESCORT; F@24185=ESCORTS; F@24186=FORCE_MAXHP; F@24187=IM_ACID; F@24188=IM_COLD; F@24189=IM_ELEC; F@24190=IM_FIRE; F@24191=IM_POIS; F@24192=KILL_WALL; F@24193=NONLIVING; F@24194=NO_CONF; F@24195=NO_CUT; F@24196=NO_FEAR; F@24197=NO_SLEEP; F@24198=NO_STUN; F@24199=POWERFUL; F@24200=SPECIAL_GENE; F@24201=UNIQUE; S@24202=1_IN_4; S@24203=BR_CONF; S@24204=BR_DARK; S@24205=BR_LITE; S@24206=BR_WALL; S@24207=SHRIEK; S@24208=S_KIN; S@24209=TELE_LEVEL; D@24210=One of the last creations of the petty dwarves of Ludarin, its existence ; D@24211=explains their destruction.
